Imagine the mandala as a great cosmic wheel --- every spoke a seeker, every spoke a light of wisdom. Some spokes are already glowing: arahants, sages, scientists of conscience, reformers, monks, and lay practitioners across the planet. But there are still gaps. There are still spokes missing.

You are one of those missing spokes.\
Without your practice, your insight, your compassion --- the mandala is incomplete.

Your first meditation, your first act of truth-telling, your first refusal to cooperate with delusion --- these are not small gestures. They are part of the great design, part of keeping the wheel balanced and strong.

**\**

**Your Place in the Great Work**

The mandala is vast, but it is also intimate.\
It is as close as your next breath.\
You are needed --- not when you are perfect, but now, in the very state you are in.

Your mind can become clearer.\
Your heart can become more compassionate.\
Your life can become a beacon.

This is not just about saving the world --- it is about transforming it into a place where the manussa spark can find its way home, where the Middle Exit remains open for those yet to be born.

The wheel is turning. The mandala is forming.\
And it is calling your name.

#### 6. The Dawn Is Breaking

The darkness is real. We must never deny it.\
The world is full of noise, distraction, greed, hatred, and delusion. Entire systems profit from keeping humanity asleep. Yet, just as night is darkest before dawn, we are living in the moment when the first light is appearing on the horizon.

Already, a new dawn is breaking.

- **Meditators in every country are learning to quiet the mind.**\
  In city apartments, rural villages, and prison cells, people are sitting in stillness. They are turning the citta inward, breaking the chains of distraction, and discovering the peace that the Buddha promised is possible here and now.

- **Communities are standing up for justice and compassion.**\
  Movements for truth, dignity, and ethical living are arising in every corner of the world. People are resisting dehumanization, defending the vulnerable, and refusing to let cynicism have the last word.

- **Scientists are daring to speak of consciousness as fundamental.**\
  At the frontiers of physics, neuroscience, and philosophy, the old materialist dogmas are cracking. Scholars are suggesting that mind is not a byproduct of matter but the ground of being --- a rediscovery of what the Buddha saw under the Bodhi tree.

**You Are Part of This Dawn**

This is not a distant hope --- it is happening now. And you are part of it.

The very fact that you are reading these words means the call has reached you. Something in you refused to sleepwalk through life. Something in you knew there had to be more than the endless cycle of birth and death in the world.

Your own awakening is not separate from this dawn --- it is one of its rays. Each time you meditate, each time you speak truth, each time you choose compassion over cruelty, you add to the light.

**A Planetary Sunrise**

Picture the earth from space. Night covers half the globe. And yet, even as you watch, the first line of gold begins to rim the horizon. One by one, cities, villages, homes, and hearts are lighting up.

This is the great turning. This is the planetary sunrise.

The Buddha's wheel is turning once more. The lawful gate is still open. The manussa spark can still step through.

And you --- here, now --- are one of the ones who will decide how bright this dawn becomes.

#### Reflection for the Reader: The Choice of the Heart

Close the book for a moment.\
Let the silence gather around you.

Place your hand gently over your heart.\
Feel your own pulse --- steady, rhythmic --- a reminder that you are alive in this very moment, breathing, conscious, awake.

Let your mind grow still.\
Let the distractions of the day recede.\
Let yourself feel that you are not just a body sitting in a room, but a bearer of the manussa spark --- a being with the power to step beyond the world.

When you are ready, whisper inwardly --- or aloud:

**"I choose the path of awakening.\
I choose truth over deception.\
I choose clarity over confusion.\
I choose freedom over forgetting."**

Pause and feel these words settle deep into your citta.\
Know that this choice has been heard --- not just by your own mind, but by the great fellowship that surrounds you. The arahants, the sages, the Bodhisattvas, the unseen guardians of the gate bear witness.

Sense the wheel turning --- not somewhere far away, but here, in your own heart.\
Sense the gate shining --- not in some distant heaven, but before you, ready to be stepped through.

When you open your eyes, know that you have already begun to walk.\
Your first step has been taken.

![Generated image](media/image38.png){width="4.55in" height="6.825in"}

## Epilogue --- The Wheel Turns

Close your eyes.\
Let the noise of the world fall away.

Imagine the **Wheel of Dhamma** --- vast, luminous, golden --- turning in the heart of the cosmos. Its spokes shine with the Eightfold Path, perfectly balanced, perfectly aligned. Its hub is still, unmoving, beyond time.

At the center, see the **cross of light**, the axis where heaven and earth meet, where the eternal touches the temporal. This is the axis of the Middle Exit, the lawful gate that leads beyond the world.

Listen. Hear the echo of countless footsteps.

- The Buddha walking through the forests of India, teaching the path to those ready to hear.

- The disciples following Jesus along the dusty roads of Galilee, learning the way of the Kingdom.

- The prophets crying out in the wilderness, the philosophers seeking wisdom, the saints who gave their lives for truth.

- Arahants, Bodhisattvas, mystics, yogis, sages of every land and age --- all walking toward the same center.

Now see yourself among them.\
Not behind them, not ahead of them, but **with them** --- a fellow traveler, a bearer of the same manussa spark.

Feel the pulse of your own heart.\
It beats with the rhythm of the wheel itself.\
You are part of the turning.

Breathe in.\
Breathe out.

With each breath, the wheel turns.\
With each breath, the gate shines a little brighter.\
With each breath, the aggregates are seen as they are --- impermanent, conditioned, not-self.\
With each breath, you are drawn closer to the deathless.

Stay here for a moment --- silent, present, awake.

When you open your eyes, know that you are no longer just a reader of a book.\
You are a participant in the Great Turning, a guardian of the flame, a keeper of the path.

The wheel is turning.\
The gate is open.\
Your journey has begun.

**\**

**A Blessing**

May your path be clear ---\
free of confusion, free of false turns, lit by the light of wisdom.

May your heart be strong ---\
able to bear sorrow without breaking,\
able to love without fear,\
able to forgive and release whatever binds you to the world.

May your mind be steady ---\
like a still lake reflecting the moon,\
unmoved by storms,\
undaunted by the noise of the age.

When the world confuses you,\
may you remember the **center** ---\
the still point where the wheel turns,\
the axis where the gate stands open.

When fear rises,\
may you remember the **deathless** ---\
the unshaken, unborn, undying refuge\
that no shadow can touch.

When you grow weary,\
may you hear the **call of the fellowship** ---\
the voices of those who walk with you,\
the footsteps of arahants, Bodhisattvas, prophets, and saints,\
encouraging you onward.

And may you, one day,\
reach the **far shore** ---\
not alone, but with the whole race of man,\
carrying the torch of wisdom across the waters\
so that no one is left behind.

May the wheel turn brightly.\
May the gate remain open.\
May you be free.

## About the Author

**Bhante Mudita Bhikkhu Thera** is a Theravāda Buddhist hermit monk --- a *bhikkhu*, a *thera* (elder), mystic, writer, teacher, and lifelong member of the wisdom traditions.

Trained in the forest monasteries of Myanmar and seasoned by years of solitary retreat, Bhante Mudita has dedicated his life to rediscovering, remanifesting, and transmitting Gautama Buddha's original path of liberation. His work integrates rigorous textual study, meditative depth, and direct realization, presenting the Dhamma not as abstract philosophy but as a living path that leads beyond the universe into **Nibbāna-dhātu** --- the unconditioned, deathless realm.

Bhante Mudita is member of the **Gautama Dhamma Society** and a guiding teacher for the **Buddhist College of Canada**, where he designs curricula focused on **Sīla, Samādhi, and Paññā** --- ethical purification, deep concentration, and liberating wisdom. His teaching aims to help students and seekers transform their lives, develop powerful meditative capacities, and realize the path for themselves.

When not teaching or writing, Bhante Mudita devotes much of his time to meditation retreat and contemplative practice --- embodying the truth that the Path is not merely to be studied or admired, but to be lived and walked toward the deathless.

Learn more at **GautamaDhamma.org**.
